# Final data and API audit

Audit date: 2026-09-16. Project: C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh.

## Scope, interpretation and counts

**88 files scanned: 52 files under expo-app/src, all 19 files under db, all 5 expo-app/*.json files, and 12 supporting entry-point, environment, Supabase configuration/function and installed SDK files.** The complete file manifest is at the end. SDK files were read only to verify the endpoint paths and configuration-error behavior used by the app; node_modules was not audited as application source.

**17 dummy-data findings (D01–D17), 4 API findings (B01–B04).** These are grouped findings, not a count of individual literals or SQL rows. D14–D17 are deliberately protected development/test fixtures; their presence is not a production leak. D09 and B03 describe the same data-access fallback from the perspectives of both requested parts. Harmless taxonomy, explicit input examples, neutral labels and correct endpoints are inventoried separately and are not counted as defects.

This is a local, static audit. No application, database query, seed, test, build, deployment or remote endpoint was executed. “Correct” below means consistent with the checked-in implementation and database definitions, conditional on deploying/configuring those definitions. Existing claims about live deployment in [db/README.md:5](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/README.md:5>) and [supabase/functions/delete-account/README.md:3](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/supabase/functions/delete-account/README.md:3>) were not treated as a fresh verification of production. Environment key values are deliberately omitted; the configured public host is included.

## Part A — remaining non-real data and business-value assumptions

### D01 — a new coach package starts at 10 sessions / $380

**Preset business data; reachable and publishable, not an existing fetched package.** The draft initializes to 10 and 38,000 cents in [expo-app/src/overlays/CoachOverlays.tsx:812](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/CoachOverlays.tsx:812>) and [expo-app/src/overlays/CoachOverlays.tsx:813](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/CoachOverlays.tsx:813>). The draft displays those values at [expo-app/src/overlays/CoachOverlays.tsx:936](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/CoachOverlays.tsx:936>) and [expo-app/src/overlays/CoachOverlays.tsx:948](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/CoachOverlays.tsx:948>). “Add package” can submit them unchanged at [expo-app/src/overlays/CoachOverlays.tsx:959](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/CoachOverlays.tsx:959>) and [expo-app/src/overlays/CoachOverlays.tsx:964](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/CoachOverlays.tsx:964>); createPackage inserts the supplied sessions and price into packages at [expo-app/src/overlays/CoachOverlays.tsx:244](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/CoachOverlays.tsx:244>). This is a visible arbitrary starting price, with an explicit user action required before it becomes a database row. The coach tools expose this screen at [expo-app/src/screens/ProfileScreen.tsx:97](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/screens/ProfileScreen.tsx:97>).

### D02 — new/anonymous accounts acquire “Beirut” without supplying a city

**Active database fallback that silently supplies a real-looking location.** users.city defaults to “Beirut” in [db/schema.sql:52](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/schema.sql:52>). The signup trigger omits city when inserting a user at [db/auth.sql:33](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/auth.sql:33>), so the default applies. Both bootstrap definitions explicitly insert “Guest”, “Beirut” and fill a null city with “Beirut”: [db/auth.sql:69](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/auth.sql:69>) and [db/hardening.sql:538](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/hardening.sql:538>). The app invokes that bootstrap for anonymous users at [expo-app/src/lib/session.ts:33](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/session.ts:33>).

“Guest” itself is an honest anonymous identity ([expo-app/src/lib/session.ts:23](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/session.ts:23>)); the city is not based on device location or user input. The onboarding location starts empty at [expo-app/src/state/store.ts:535](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/store.ts:535>), and its UI explicitly treats the area as a display label at [expo-app/src/screens/AuthLanding.tsx:129](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/screens/AuthLanding.tsx:129>). Thus the cleaned-up onboarding label does not establish the database city.

### D03 — venue/court adapters and schema supply real-looking operating facts

**Conditional client fallbacks plus active database defaults; silently wrong when the venue's actual facts differ.** [expo-app/src/lib/courts.ts:134](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/courts.ts:134>) defaults any status other than “closed” to “open”; [expo-app/src/lib/courts.ts:137](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/courts.ts:137>) supplies 09:00:00, [expo-app/src/lib/courts.ts:138](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/courts.ts:138>) supplies 22:00:00, [expo-app/src/lib/courts.ts:139](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/courts.ts:139>) supplies Asia/Beirut, and [expo-app/src/lib/courts.ts:148](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/courts.ts:148>) supplies capacity 2. Reservation mapping supplies hours 1 and booking kind “single” at [expo-app/src/lib/courts.ts:219](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/courts.ts:219>).

The selected database columns are normally NOT NULL, so these client null fallbacks should not be reached with a conforming row: [db/migrations/2026-09-03_venues.sql:60](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/migrations/2026-09-03_venues.sql:60>), :65, :67, :68, :71, :92, :135 and :144. However, that schema itself defaults venue city to Beirut, status to open, operating days to every day, hours to 09:00–22:00, timezone to Asia/Beirut and court capacity to 2 ([db/migrations/2026-09-03_venues.sql:54](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/migrations/2026-09-03_venues.sql:54>), :60, :65, :66, :67, :68, :71 and :92). An insert omitting these facts therefore persists assumptions as real venue data. The city/hours/status are displayed at [expo-app/src/screens/CourtsScreen.tsx:312](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/screens/CourtsScreen.tsx:312>) and [expo-app/src/screens/CourtsScreen.tsx:319](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/screens/CourtsScreen.tsx:319>); capacity is used in [expo-app/src/state/courtsData.ts:51](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/courtsData.ts:51>).

**Reachability qualification:** CourtsScreen remains mounted for a “courts” tab in [expo-app/src/navigation/Root.tsx:79](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/navigation/Root.tsx:79>), but that tab is absent from the rendered tab list at [expo-app/src/navigation/TabBar.tsx:14](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/navigation/TabBar.tsx:14>). The defaults still exist in the database and source.

### D04 — the retained Accounting console simulates a ledger and approvals

**Stale simulation, deliberately removed from ordinary navigation.** Its revenue is a constant zero from [expo-app/src/state/sampleData.ts:29](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/sampleData.ts:29>), consumed at [expo-app/src/state/store.ts:1155](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/store.ts:1155>); margins start at zero and shares/expenses/history start empty at [expo-app/src/state/store.ts:636](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/store.ts:636>). Zero is neutral initialization, not a fabricated positive revenue, but the retained UI labels it “This month”, “Revenue” and “Net profit” at [expo-app/src/overlays/AccountingOverlays.tsx:33](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/AccountingOverlays.tsx:33>), :37 and :56 without reading a ledger.

User-entered expense objects and history are only local state ([expo-app/src/state/store.ts:1262](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/store.ts:1262>) and [expo-app/src/state/store.ts:1276](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/store.ts:1276>)). Recurrence descriptions promise automatic weekly/monthly/yearly re-addition at [expo-app/src/state/sampleData.ts:42](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/sampleData.ts:42>), while the expense form renders those descriptions at [expo-app/src/overlays/AccountingOverlays.tsx:188](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/AccountingOverlays.tsx:188>).

The local approval simulation creates “Approved by 3 admins”, “affected users notified”, “change is live” and a platform-fee notification, without a database or notification write ([expo-app/src/state/store.ts:1216](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/store.ts:1216>), :1227, :1235 and :1239). “Just now” is stored as a fixed history label rather than a timestamp at [expo-app/src/state/store.ts:1224](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/store.ts:1224>) and :1271.

There is no populated fake admin list: allAdmins and shareDefs are empty at [expo-app/src/state/sampleData.ts:35](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/sampleData.ts:35>). The only approval controls enumerate that empty list at [expo-app/src/overlays/AccountingOverlays.tsx:101](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/AccountingOverlays.tsx:101>). The navigation removal and its reason are explicit at [expo-app/src/screens/ProfileScreen.tsx:157](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/screens/ProfileScreen.tsx:157>); router cases remain at [expo-app/src/navigation/OverlayRouter.tsx:102](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/navigation/OverlayRouter.tsx:102>). This is dormant residue, not an active three-admin workflow.

### D06 — dormant promo generators retain “ALEX” and “SPOT”

**Stale sample identity/branding in locally generated promo data, no current UI callers found.** genCoachPromo generates “ALEX-<random>-<percent>” at [expo-app/src/state/store.ts:1326](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/store.ts:1326>); genPromo generates “SPOT<percent>-<random>” at [expo-app/src/state/store.ts:1303](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/store.ts:1303>). These only set local strings. The current saved generators use “<percent>OFF-<random>” in coach_promos at [expo-app/src/overlays/CoachOverlays.tsx:285](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/CoachOverlays.tsx:285>) and “BOOKD<percent>-<random>” in platform_promos at [expo-app/src/overlays/AdminOverlays.tsx:78](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/AdminOverlays.tsx:78>). ### D07 — dormant certificate creation stamps every certificate “2026”

**Stale entity construction, no current UI caller found.** addCert constructs an object with issuer “Awaiting verification”, year “2026” and verified false at [expo-app/src/state/store.ts:1298](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/store.ts:1298>). The supplied certificate name is user input; the fixed year is not. myCerts starts empty ([expo-app/src/state/store.ts:650](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/store.ts:650>)). No initial fake certificate is loaded.

### D08 — orphan CommunityRegisterOverlay fabricates an uploaded document and submission

**Stale mock flow, replaced in the active router.** Its upload control simply sets the filename “federation-charter.pdf” ([expo-app/src/overlays/CommunityRegisterOverlay.tsx:90](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/CommunityRegisterOverlay.tsx:90>)) and renders it as the document label ([expo-app/src/overlays/CommunityRegisterOverlay.tsx:104](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/CommunityRegisterOverlay.tsx:104>)). The submit button only sets sent=true ([expo-app/src/overlays/CommunityRegisterOverlay.tsx:47](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/CommunityRegisterOverlay.tsx:47>)), followed by “Request sent” and an admin-contact promise ([expo-app/src/overlays/CommunityRegisterOverlay.tsx:33](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/CommunityRegisterOverlay.tsx:33>)).

Both registration and communityRegister route to RegistrationOverlay instead at [expo-app/src/navigation/OverlayRouter.tsx:52](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/navigation/OverlayRouter.tsx:52>). The current community/venue flow records user input locally and explicitly says it has not been sent ([expo-app/src/overlays/RegistrationOverlay.tsx:37](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/RegistrationOverlay.tsx:37>), :45, :105 and :115). The mock filename and mock confirmation remain only in the orphan implementation.

### D09 — package read fallback can invent a new purchase; entitlement display uses the current listing

**Active silent misbehavior involving quoted prices/counts; also B03.** fetchPackageUsage selects only package_id and used, then catches every failure and returns an empty map ([expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:422](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:422>), :428 and :436). BookingOverlay initializes usage to an empty map and treats absent usage as zero ([expo-app/src/overlays/BookingOverlay.tsx:28](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/BookingOverlay.tsx:28>) and :35). This can show the full package price while loading or after a failed read, including in the post-booking “payable to coach” text ([expo-app/src/overlays/BookingOverlay.tsx:39](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/BookingOverlay.tsx:39>) and :61), even when the server records a zero-cost redemption.

The remaining-session display also subtracts used from the package's current listing sessions, not the purchased balance total ([expo-app/src/overlays/BookingOverlay.tsx:36](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/BookingOverlay.tsx:36>)). The server deliberately retains purchased total when the listing changes ([db/migrations/2026-09-16_enforce_coach_availability.sql:78](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/migrations/2026-09-16_enforce_coach_availability.sql:78>) and :108). Thus the app can claim “Pack already used” or a wrong remaining count after a coach edits the listing ([expo-app/src/overlays/BookingOverlay.tsx:83](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/BookingOverlay.tsx:83>) and :92). No hardcoded nonzero seed is involved, but the fallback/mapping presents non-real business data.

### D10 — parked Shop display rounds real cents to invented whole-dollar prices

**Stale price transformation in parked code.** fromRemoteShop uses Math.round(price_cents / 100) at [expo-app/src/screens/ShopScreen.tsx:99](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/screens/ShopScreen.tsx:99>). For example, 1,899 stored cents displays as $19 rather than $18.99; that rounded value also enters the local cart through [expo-app/src/overlays/ShopOverlays.tsx:89](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/ShopOverlays.tsx:89>) and [expo-app/src/state/store.ts:946](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/store.ts:946>). The checkout RPC reads the product's actual price_cents at [db/auth.sql:289](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/auth.sql:289>). It therefore need not match the displayed/cart total. The same adapter uses zero for a missing price; the database's product price is required at [db/schema.sql:406](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/schema.sql:406>), so missing prices are not normal conforming rows.

Shop is explicitly parked in [expo-app/src/navigation/Root.tsx:82](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/navigation/Root.tsx:82>); the root renders no ShopScreen and the router contains no shop overlay cases ([expo-app/src/navigation/Root.tsx:77](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/navigation/Root.tsx:77>) and [expo-app/src/navigation/OverlayRouter.tsx:44](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/navigation/OverlayRouter.tsx:44>)).

### D11 — parked Shop captions imply an applied deal and fulfilment without that data

**Stale business assertions in parked code.** A null deal_text becomes “In-app checkout” at [expo-app/src/screens/ShopScreen.tsx:115](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/screens/ShopScreen.tsx:115>); the overlay appends “applied at in-app checkout” to the deal at [expo-app/src/overlays/ShopOverlays.tsx:73](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/ShopOverlays.tsx:73>). checkout_shop_order only totals products and commission, with no coupon/discount application ([db/auth.sql:283](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/auth.sql:283>) and :295). “The store will prepare it for pickup or delivery” is also fixed confirmation text at [expo-app/src/overlays/ShopOverlays.tsx:26](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/ShopOverlays.tsx:26>), whereas the write creates an order with status placed ([db/auth.sql:284](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/auth.sql:284>)). These are not fetched fulfilment or discount facts. Parking evidence: [expo-app/src/navigation/Root.tsx:82](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/navigation/Root.tsx:82>).

### D12 — an “official” community is described as a federation

**Conditional unsupported entity classification.** [expo-app/src/overlays/CommunityProfileOverlay.tsx:62](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/CommunityProfileOverlay.tsx:62>) renders “Official Federation” solely from cm.official. The community model contains an official boolean but no federation/type field ([expo-app/src/state/models.ts:73](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/models.ts:73>)), and the queried database fields likewise have only official ([expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:190](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:190>); [db/schema.sql:257](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/schema.sql:257>)). The same profile prefixes every community with “Sports,” at [expo-app/src/overlays/CommunityProfileOverlay.tsx:65](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/CommunityProfileOverlay.tsx:65>) even though the taxonomy includes Music and Chess ([expo-app/src/state/sampleData.ts:6](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/sampleData.ts:6>)).

### D13 — a shop registration promises contact within two business days

**Unverified fixed business promise; source does not establish that it is real.** The shared form's shop copy promises admin contact “within 2 business days” at [expo-app/src/overlays/RegistrationOverlay.tsx:52](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/RegistrationOverlay.tsx:52>). Submission stores a request via submit_shop_registration ([expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:374](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:374>); [db/auth.sql:258](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/auth.sql:258>)), with no deadline/contact scheduling in that routine. This is not proof that a human operating policy is false; it is a literal SLA not verified by the repository. The normal Shop destination is parked ([expo-app/src/navigation/Root.tsx:82](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/navigation/Root.tsx:82>)); the shared registration component and shop branch remain ([expo-app/src/navigation/OverlayRouter.tsx:52](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/navigation/OverlayRouter.tsx:52>); [expo-app/src/overlays/RegistrationOverlay.tsx:92](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/RegistrationOverlay.tsx:92>)).

### D14 — db/seed.sql contains a full demo catalogue

**Demo/test entity data; correctly blocked by default, unsafe as real production inventory.** The file opens a transaction and raises unless bookd.demo_seed is explicitly “on” ([db/seed.sql:8](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/seed.sql:8>) and :11). Automatic CLI seeding is disabled with an empty seed path list ([supabase/config.toml:66](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/supabase/config.toml:66>)). All 40 INSERT sites are listed below, including the INSERT inside the conversation CTE.

The fixture includes 21 named accounts and sample emails, all assigned Beirut ([db/seed.sql:49](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/seed.sql:49>)); eight coaches with invented prices, ratings, reviews, session counts, biographies, reply times, boosts and verification ([db/seed.sql:89](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/seed.sql:89>)); fabricated member/attendance counts ([db/seed.sql:150](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/seed.sql:150>) and :177); dated bookings, balances and coupons ([db/seed.sql:134](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/seed.sql:134>), :138 and :214); fake reviews, messages, ads, notifications, promos, loyalty balances, expenses and moderation records (see the complete INSERT inventory). These are not app fallback arrays, but would become normal queryable rows if this development file were intentionally enabled.

There is also a non-INSERT mutation: “update coach_profiles set subscription_status='active'” applies to **every existing coach profile**, not only fixture coaches ([db/seed.sql:101](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/seed.sql:101>)). The opt-in guard protects it too. The file is not wholly idempotent: many statements have no conflict handling, e.g. packages at [db/seed.sql:104](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/seed.sql:104>) and messages at [db/seed.sql:233](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/seed.sql:233>).

### D16 — release_invariants.sql builds explicit test entities and aborts them

**Deliberate test fixtures, correct isolation in the whole file.** [db/tests/release_invariants.sql:45](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/tests/release_invariants.sql:45>) inserts an auth user, :53 renames its app profile to “ZZ Invariant Client”, :55 and :56 insert “ZZ Invariant Other/Coach”, :58 adds a 5,000-cent coach profile, :61 adds a five-session 20,000-cent package, :66 adds ZZ Invariant Venue in Beirut with UTC/00:00–23:59/500-cent equipment, and :72 adds ZZ COURT at 3,000 cents. There is another 1,000-cent coach profile at :184, messages “should not arrive” and “ok” at :219 and :236, and a 6:30 PM availability fixture at :290.

The test also invokes real mutation routines to create bookings/reservations/conversations/blocks/notifications using its fixture IDs ([db/tests/release_invariants.sql:89](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/tests/release_invariants.sql:89>), :113, :197, :213 and :245). Everything is inside one DO block ([db/tests/release_invariants.sql:29](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/tests/release_invariants.sql:29>)), which unconditionally raises at :345; normal success and failure both roll back that block's writes. The audit did not run it.

### D17 — check_release_defaults.sql creates a temporary “Release check …” community

**Deliberate rollback-only fixture, correct when the complete script is used.** The script begins a transaction ([db/check_release_defaults.sql:3](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/check_release_defaults.sql:3>)), selects an existing auth-linked account and sets transaction-local claims ([db/check_release_defaults.sql:13](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/check_release_defaults.sql:13>)), invokes bootstrap ([db/check_release_defaults.sql:20](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/check_release_defaults.sql:20>)), and creates “Release check <UUID>” through create_community_with_owner ([db/check_release_defaults.sql:28](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/check_release_defaults.sql:28>)). There are no direct INSERT statements in this file; its writes are through those routines. It explicitly rolls back at [db/check_release_defaults.sql:37](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/check_release_defaults.sql:37>). The bootstrap's city behavior is still D02, including on this test path before rollback.

## Part B — API, hosts and connections

### B01 — missing client environment values select localhost / a fake key

**Broken production configuration fallback, not a production backend.** [expo-app/src/lib/supabase.ts:9](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/supabase.ts:9>) reads EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL, :10 reads EXPO_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY, and :15 creates the client with “http://localhost” / “anon” when the respective value is undefined. isSupabaseConfigured is only a Boolean presence check at :12.

ensureAppSession explicitly throws “Supabase is not configured” at [expo-app/src/lib/session.ts:14](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/session.ts:14>), so paths through that guard fail clearly. Public browsing reads do not all use that guard: fetchCoaches begins its query at [expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:124](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:124>) and fetchPartners at :153. Email sign-in/signup and SSO also directly use the client ([expo-app/src/lib/session.ts:50](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/session.ts:50>), :56 and :120). Those paths can target the fallback host or use the fake key. With an absent URL, the separate provider probe uses an empty supabaseUrl prefix, yielding /auth/v1/authorize on web ([expo-app/src/lib/supabase.ts:13](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/supabase.ts:13>); [expo-app/src/lib/session.ts:108](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/session.ts:108>)).

Empty strings are a different failure: nullish coalescing does not replace them ([expo-app/src/lib/supabase.ts:15](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/supabase.ts:15>)), and the SDK throws for an empty URL/key during initialization ([expo-app/node_modules/@supabase/supabase-js/src/lib/helpers.ts:107](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/node_modules/@supabase/supabase-js/src/lib/helpers.ts:107>); [expo-app/node_modules/@supabase/supabase-js/src/SupabaseClient.ts:315](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/node_modules/@supabase/supabase-js/src/SupabaseClient.ts:315>)). A nonempty placeholder URL/key passes the app's presence check; format checking is not project/key authentication ([expo-app/src/lib/supabase.ts:12](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/supabase.ts:12>); [expo-app/node_modules/@supabase/supabase-js/src/lib/helpers.ts:113](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/node_modules/@supabase/supabase-js/src/lib/helpers.ts:113>)).

The current local .env has a hosted URL and a key assignment ([expo-app/.env:3](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/.env:3>) and :4). This does not prove the same values are present in an EAS production build; production selects its own environment at [expo-app/eas.json:17](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/eas.json:17>).

### B02 — sport_requests exists, but its admin UPDATE is not granted by the repository

**Broken against the checked-in hardening permissions; not a missing table.** decideSportRequest writes status and reviewed_by directly to sport_requests ([expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:319](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:319>) and :323). The table exists ([db/schema.sql:103](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/schema.sql:103>)) and an admin RLS policy exists ([db/policies.sql:56](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/policies.sql:56>)), but [db/hardening.sql:17](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/hardening.sql:17>) revokes INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE from authenticated and anon. The replacement grant is INSERT only for sport_requests ([db/hardening.sql:495](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/hardening.sql:495>)). No later sport_requests UPDATE grant was found in the scanned db definitions/migrations; the full DB file manifest is below.

An application role “ADMIN” is still an authenticated database client, not a service-role connection ([expo-app/src/lib/supabase.ts:10](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/supabase.ts:10>) and :15; [expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:402](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:402>)). The RLS policy alone cannot restore SQL UPDATE privilege. The source therefore predicts permission denial when an admin approves/rejects, unless the live database has an additional grant not present here. The client does surface the failed update rather than silently displaying success ([expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:328](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:328>); [expo-app/src/overlays/AdminApprovalsOverlay.tsx:33](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/AdminApprovalsOverlay.tsx:33>)).

### B03 — client_package_balances read failure is converted to an empty business answer

**Active silent fallback.** The table and selected columns exist ([db/schema.sql:243](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/schema.sql:243>)); the query explicitly scopes client_id ([expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:427](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:427>)). However, its catch returns {} for every error ([expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:436](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:436>)), with no error/loading state exposed to BookingOverlay, which interprets absent usage as zero ([expo-app/src/overlays/BookingOverlay.tsx:28](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/overlays/BookingOverlay.tsx:28>) and :35). The resulting wrong quote/remaining-session implications are detailed in D09. Endpoint naming is correct; failure semantics are not reliable for production pricing text.

### B04 — unused fetchMyRole reads any membership, then silently defaults to member

**Stale helper; would silently misbehave if used.** [expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:233](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:233>) selects a community's role without filtering user_id, calls maybeSingle, discards error and returns “member” if data is absent. community_members is readable under a broad read policy ([db/policies.sql:69](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/policies.sql:69>)). A community with multiple memberships can yield a maybeSingle error; a single membership can belong to somebody else. This is not a valid current-user role lookup.

The source-wide caller search found no usage beyond the definition at [expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:233](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:233>). The live membership loader instead resolves the real public user ID and filters user_id ([expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:218](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:218>) and :227), and the store applies those memberships at [expo-app/src/state/store.ts:770](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/state/store.ts:770>). Thus B04 is dormant, not evidence that the current community screen uses the broken lookup.

There are **exactly two process.env reads** in expo-app/src, both above. No environment-variable read occurs in the five JSON files; EAS environment names “preview” and “production” are configuration selections ([expo-app/eas.json:8](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/eas.json:8>) and :18), not additional application variables.

The Edge Function reads **Deno.env**, not process.env:
- SUPABASE_URL at [supabase/functions/delete-account/index.ts:46](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/supabase/functions/delete-account/index.ts:46>) supplies both server clients.
- SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY at [supabase/functions/delete-account/index.ts:47](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/supabase/functions/delete-account/index.ts:47>) supplies the admin client at :58.
- SUPABASE_ANON_KEY at [supabase/functions/delete-account/index.ts:51](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/supabase/functions/delete-account/index.ts:51>) supplies the caller client.

All three use TypeScript non-null assertions, which do not provide a runtime fallback. Missing server values lead to client initialization failure; there is no enclosing try/catch around construction ([supabase/functions/delete-account/index.ts:37](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/supabase/functions/delete-account/index.ts:37>) and :46). These are correct server configuration dependencies, not client secrets or dummy connection strings.

The inspected Supabase CLI configuration also references provider client IDs/secrets through env(...), separately from app reads: Apple at [supabase/config.toml:354](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/supabase/config.toml:354>)/:356, Facebook at :369/:370, Google at :378/:379 and Azure at :389/:390. It also references OPENAI_API_KEY for local Studio at :101, a disabled Twilio token at :306, and S3_HOST/S3_REGION/S3_ACCESS_KEY/S3_SECRET_KEY at :459/:461/:463/:465. Those values and hosted provider setup were not available from this read-only source audit; they are not fallback business data or hardcoded live credentials.

### Edge Function inventory

**One invoked function: delete-account**, called with POST at [expo-app/src/lib/session.ts:167](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/session.ts:167>). **Present locally:** [supabase/functions/delete-account/index.ts:37](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/supabase/functions/delete-account/index.ts:37>) handles requests, :39 rejects methods other than POST, :41 requires a bearer header and :54 validates the caller with Auth. An implementation is present; deployment/version/remote availability were not reverified. ### Table and RPC existence comparison

The following inventories enumerate every direct .from call, every named callRpc/supabase.rpc call and their definitions. All **27 distinct direct table names** exist in db/schema.sql or db/migrations. All **20 distinct named RPCs** exist somewhere in db. **Eleven RPC names are absent from schema.sql + migrations alone, but are defined in db/auth.sql and/or db/hardening.sql**; those prerequisites are explicitly identified below instead of being mislabeled as missing from the repository.

The app's two dynamic RPC forwarding sites are [expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:18](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:18>) and [expo-app/src/lib/courts.ts:232](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/courts.ts:232>). Their concrete callers are included below; there are no other unresolved runtime function names in the src scan. The booking call supplies p_coach, p_scheduled_for, p_slot_label, p_package_id and p_slot ([expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:356](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/expo-app/src/lib/queries.ts:356>)), matching the five-argument definition at [db/migrations/2026-09-16_enforce_coach_availability.sql:27](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/migrations/2026-09-16_enforce_coach_availability.sql:27>). Older four-argument implementations also remain in the migration sources, including [db/migrations/2026-09-16_package_booking_race_and_entitlement.sql:28](<C:/Users/kasse/Documents/Claued proj/Book-a-sesh/db/migrations/2026-09-16_package_booking_race_and_entitlement.sql:28>); the five-argument app call is not evidence that those older signatures are missing.

Production assessment in these inventories concerns endpoint/definition compatibility, not a guarantee of live connectivity or every business rule. B01 applies to all client calls; B02/B03/B04 are marked on the affected table entries.
